-- althea-tech.ru — РАСШИРЕННЫЙ SQL КАРКАС v6.0 (полное зеркало сайта)
-- Дата: 2026-08-12
--
-- ИЗМЕНЕНИЯ v6.0 vs v5.0 (за 2026-08-12):
--   1. index.html: title приведён к стандарту с data-ru/data-en атрибутами
--   2. index.html: добавлены полные OG/Twitter Card мета-теги (как на services.html)
--   3. index.html: добавлен canonical, og:image 1200x630, og:image:alt
--   4. index.html: удалены 13 дубликатов старых мета-тегов (с self-closing />)
--   5. news.html: CSS-цвета приведены к стандарту:
--      - cookies p color: var(--muted) → var(--amagi)
--      - cookies p a color: var(--scio) → var(--amagi)
--      - dd-arrow color: var(--faint) → var(--amagi)
--      - board-top .live color: var(--althea) → var(--amagi)
--   6. news.html: emoji-иконки заменены на оригинальные SVG бренда:
--      - 🟡 AMAGI → amagi.svg (щит + чип + circuit traces, цвет #F5A623)
--      - 🟢 ALTHEA → althea.svg (ДНК спираль + CRISPR cut, цвет #2DD4A7)
--      - 🔵 Scio → scio.svg (глаз + scan-line + brackets, цвет #4CC3E8)
--      - 🟣 CTC → ctc.svg (hub + 5 spokes + ring, цвет #C084FC)
--      - 📚 Наука → SVG иконка книги (цвет #4CC3E8)
--      - ✉️ Контакты → SVG иконка конверта (цвет #7AA2C8)
--   7. Footer: подтверждена идентичность на всех 23 страницах
--      (только index.html имеет href="#top" вместо href="index.html")
--   8. Nav: подтверждена идентичность на всех 23 страницах
--   9. Создан althea-deploy-v2.zip с 36 файлами для деплоя
--  10. PWA иконки: icon-192.png, icon-512.png, icon-maskable-*.png загружены
--  11. og-image.png 1200x630 загружен (замена apple-touch-icon.png 180x180)
--  12. site.webmanifest обновлён с 7 иконками + start_url, scope, categories
-- Назначение: single source of truth — каждая страница, кнопка, ссылка, секция
--
-- ЧЕРЕЗ API можно получить:
--   SELECT * FROM pages WHERE slug = 'services'          — полная инфо о странице
--   SELECT * FROM page_buttons WHERE page_slug = 'index' — все кнопки на главной
--   SELECT * FROM navigation_items WHERE is_active = 1   — меню шапки
--   SELECT * FROM footer_links ORDER BY column_name      — футер ссылки
--   SELECT * FROM api_endpoints WHERE is_active = 1      — все PHP API
--   SELECT * FROM cron_jobs                              — все CRON задачи
--   SELECT * FROM promotion_channels                     — каналы продвижения
--   SELECT * FROM canonical_values                       — канонические значения
--   SELECT * FROM compliance_markers                     — правовые маркеры
--
-- СТАТИСТИКА:
--   pages: 23 | nav_items: 17 | footer_links: 29
--   buttons: 123 | sections: 132 | external_links: 132
--   forms: 2 | api_endpoints: 18 | cron_jobs: 13
--   assets: 13 | promotion_channels: 8 | telegram_bots: 3
--
-- ИЗМЕНЕНИЯ v5.0 vs v4.0 (за 2026-08-06..07):
--   1. AI→ИИ: 31 англицизм заменён на ИИ в русском контенте (services, careers,
--      team, vision, outstaffing, index, news, bot, regulations, oferta + JSON-LD)
--   2. HTML attribute quotes: 19 внутренних кавычек экранировано (&quot; в data-ru/data-en)
--   3. Translation completeness: 100% (0 ru-no-en на всех 23 страницах)
--   4. Menu hover fix: position:relative + ::before bridge (12px) + visibility delay 150ms
--   5. Menu dropdown arrow: ▾ индикатор добавлен на всех 23 страницах (через premium.css)
--   6. Active menu highlight: JS-функция initActiveMenu() подсвечивает текущую страницу
--   7. News page (news.html): добавлена setLang(), локализация категорий, empty/error states
--   8. 53-ФЗ ссылка исправлена: consultant.ru cons_doc_LAW_366612 → cons_doc_LAW_53749
--   9. Premium assets bump: premium.css?v=6, premium-v7.js
--  10. regulations.html: восстановлен из бэкапа, исправлен сломанный div с двойным data-en
--  11. architecture.html, consent.html: добавлен premium.css (ранее отсутствовал)
-- ============================================================================

PRAGMA foreign_keys = OFF;
BEGIN TRANSACTION;

-- ============================================================
-- 1. PAGES (расширенная таблица — 23 страницы)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pages (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    slug TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    filename TEXT NOT NULL,
    title TEXT NOT NULL,
    meta_description TEXT,
    canonical TEXT,
    h1 TEXT,
    og_title TEXT,
    og_description TEXT,
    og_type TEXT DEFAULT 'website',
    og_url TEXT,
    twitter_card TEXT DEFAULT 'summary_large_image',
    lang TEXT DEFAULT 'ru',
    file_size INTEGER,
    word_count INTEGER,
    jsonld_blocks INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    button_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    external_link_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    image_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    form_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    section_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    is_published INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    page_category TEXT,
    sort_order INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    last_modified TEXT,
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
    updated_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

-- ============================================================
-- 2. NAVIGATION ITEMS (header nav — 17 пунктов)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS navigation_items (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    href TEXT NOT NULL,
    text_ru TEXT NOT NULL,
    text_en TEXT,
    type TEXT DEFAULT 'link',
    parent TEXT,
    code TEXT,
    sort_order INTEGER NOT NULL,
    is_active INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    style TEXT,
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

-- ============================================================
-- 3. FOOTER LINKS (29 ссылок в 4 колонках)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS footer_links (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    column_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    href TEXT NOT NULL,
    text TEXT NOT NULL,
    text_en TEXT,
    is_external INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    sort_order INTEGER NOT NULL,
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

-- ============================================================
-- 4. PAGE BUTTONS (123 кнопки — текст + URL)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS page_buttons (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    page_slug TEXT NOT NULL,
    text TEXT NOT NULL,
    href TEXT NOT NULL,
    button_class TEXT,
    is_external INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    sort_order INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
    FOREIGN KEY (page_slug) REFERENCES pages(slug) ON DELETE CASCADE
);

-- ============================================================
-- 5. PAGE SECTIONS (132 секции — заголовки h2)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS page_sections (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    page_slug TEXT NOT NULL,
    heading TEXT NOT NULL,
    heading_level TEXT DEFAULT 'h2',
    sort_order INTEGER NOT NULL,
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
    FOREIGN KEY (page_slug) REFERENCES pages(slug) ON DELETE CASCADE
);

-- ============================================================
-- 6. PAGE EXTERNAL LINKS (132 внешние ссылки)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS page_external_links (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    page_slug TEXT NOT NULL,
    domain TEXT NOT NULL,
    full_url TEXT,
    link_type TEXT DEFAULT 'link',
    rel TEXT,
    target TEXT DEFAULT '_blank',
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
    FOREIGN KEY (page_slug) REFERENCES pages(slug) ON DELETE CASCADE
);

-- ============================================================
-- 7. PAGE FORMS (2 формы)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS page_forms (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    page_slug TEXT NOT NULL,
    form_id TEXT,
    action TEXT NOT NULL,
    method TEXT DEFAULT 'POST',
    fields TEXT,
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
    FOREIGN KEY (page_slug) REFERENCES pages(slug) ON DELETE CASCADE
);

-- ============================================================
-- 8. API ENDPOINTS (18 PHP файлов)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS api_endpoints (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    filename TEXT NOT NULL,
    path TEXT NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    requires_key INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    methods TEXT DEFAULT 'GET',
    is_active INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    is_in_cron INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    cron_schedule TEXT,
    file_size INTEGER,
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

-- ============================================================
-- 9. CRON JOBS (13 задач)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS cron_jobs (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    schedule TEXT NOT NULL,
    command TEXT NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    script_type TEXT,
    log_file TEXT,
    is_active INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

-- ============================================================
-- 10. ASSETS (favicons, icons, JS, CSS) — 13 файлов
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS assets (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    filename TEXT NOT NULL,
    path TEXT NOT NULL,
    asset_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    file_size INTEGER,
    description TEXT,
    is_active INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

-- ============================================================
-- 11. PROMOTION CHANNELS (8 каналов)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS promotion_channels (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    name TEXT NOT NULL,
    type TEXT NOT NULL,
    url TEXT,
    api_endpoint TEXT,
    is_active INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    last_response_code INTEGER,
    description TEXT,
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

-- ============================================================
-- 12. TELEGRAM BOTS (3 бота)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS telegram_bots (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    bot_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    bot_username TEXT,
    script_path TEXT,
    description TEXT,
    is_active INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    pid INTEGER,
    cron_schedule TEXT,
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

-- ============================================================
-- 13. COMPLIANCE MARKERS (правовые маркеры)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS compliance_markers (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    marker_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    marker_value TEXT NOT NULL,
    law TEXT,
    description TEXT,
    is_active INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

-- ============================================================
-- 14. CANONICAL VALUES (из IEEE TCAD + Zenodo)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS canonical_values (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    key_name TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    value TEXT NOT NULL,
    unit TEXT,
    source TEXT,
    description TEXT,
    updated_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

-- ============================================================
-- 15. SITE SETTINGS (расширенные — 57 ключей)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS site_settings (
    key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    value TEXT,
    type TEXT DEFAULT 'text',
    description TEXT,
    updated_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

-- ============================================================
-- PAGES DATA (23 страницы)
-- ============================================================

-- ============================================================
-- 16. YOUTUBE SHORTS PIPELINE (v4.1 — автоматическая генерация видео)
-- ============================================================
-- Репозиторий: github.com/AGIAMTech/althea-shorts (private)
-- Канал: https://www.youtube.com/@ALTHEAResearchBrief
-- Pipeline: edge-tts (TTS) → v4_visual (5 сцен) → v4_subtitles (captions) → v4_compose (FFmpeg)
-- Cron: каждые 4 часа через GitHub Actions
-- ============================================================

-- 16.1 Категории (6 brand pillars)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS youtube_categories (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
    slug TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    label TEXT NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    is_active INTEGER DEFAULT 1
);

INSERT INTO youtube_categories (id, slug, label, description) VALUES
(1, 'amagi',       'AMAGI',       'Hardware security for AI — formal verification, side-channel attacks'),
(2, 'althea',      'ALTHEA',      'Bioengineering — CRISPR, synthetic biology, gene therapy'),
(3, 'scio',        'Scio',        'Deepfake detection — media forensics, content authenticity'),
(4, 'ctc',         'CTC',         'Clinical Trial Coordination — AI therapy orchestration'),
(5, 'industry',    'Industry',    'Deep-tech industry news — semiconductors, AI chips, robotics'),
(6, 'publication', 'Publication', 'Scientific publications — peer-reviewed research, arXiv papers');

-- 16.2 TTS голоса по категориям (edge-tts, free Microsoft Neural TTS)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS youtube_tts_voices (
    category_slug TEXT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES youtube_categories(slug),
    voice_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    voice_gender TEXT NOT NULL,
    voice_description TEXT,
    rate TEXT NOT NULL,
    pitch TEXT NOT NULL,
    notes TEXT
);

INSERT INTO youtube_tts_voices (category_slug, voice_id, voice_gender, voice_description, rate, pitch, notes) VALUES
('amagi',       'ru-RU-DmitryNeural',   'male',   'Dmitry — serious, authoritative', '-5%',  '-2Hz', 'Slower for technical hardware content'),
('althea',      'ru-RU-SvetlanaNeural', 'female', 'Svetlana — warm, scientific',      '+0%',  '+0Hz', 'Female voice for bio/medical content'),
('scio',        'ru-RU-DmitryNeural',   'male',   'Dmitry — mysterious, investigative','-8%', '-3Hz', 'Slowest + lower pitch for forensics'),
('ctc',         'ru-RU-SvetlanaNeural', 'female', 'Svetlana — neutral medical',      '+0%',  '+0Hz', 'Female voice, neutral pace for clinical'),
('industry',    'ru-RU-DmitryNeural',   'male',   'Dmitry — energetic, fast',        '+5%',  '+2Hz', 'Faster pace for industry news'),
('publication', 'ru-RU-DmitryNeural',   'male',   'Dmitry — neutral academic',       '+0%',  '+0Hz', 'Neutral pace for research papers');

-- 16.3 Фоновая музыка (royalty-free, Kevin MacLeod CC BY 4.0)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS youtube_music_tracks (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
    filename TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    title TEXT NOT NULL,
    artist TEXT DEFAULT 'Kevin MacLeod',
    source_url TEXT DEFAULT 'incompetech.com',
    license TEXT DEFAULT 'CC BY 4.0',
    license_url TEXT DEFAULT 'https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/',
    mood TEXT,
    used_for_categories TEXT
);

);

-- 16.14 TTS workarounds (edge-tts 7.x bugs)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS youtube_tts_workarounds (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
    bug_description TEXT NOT NULL,
    workaround TEXT NOT NULL,
    file_affected TEXT
);

INSERT INTO youtube_tts_workarounds (id, bug_description, workaround, file_affected) VALUES
(1, 'Long text with boundary=WordBoundary returns NoAudioReceived', 'Chunk text at sentence boundaries (<180 chars)', 'v4_subtitles.py:_tts_with_word_boundaries'),
(2, 'WordBoundary events are phrase-level (e.g. "7 августа 2026" as one event)', 'Split multi-word events into single words with proportional duration by char count', 'v4_subtitles.py:_tts_with_word_boundaries'),
(3, 'Period in "ТЕХ.РУ." causes 1.3s pause before "РУ" (TTS treats as end-of-sentence)', 'Use "АЛТЕЯ ТЕХ РУ" (no period between ТЕХ and РУ)', 'llm_filter.py, youtube_shorts.py'),
(4, 'Byte-count heuristic for chunk duration off by 50% (17s drift on 32s audio)', 'Use ffprobe to measure real chunk duration', 'v4_subtitles.py:_tts_with_word_boundaries'),
(5, 'Caption overlap (PHRASE_TAIL_MS + |CAPTION_LEAD_MS| = 330ms overlap)', 'PHRASE_TAIL_MS=0 + anti-overlap post-processing (clamp end_ms to next start_ms)', 'v4_subtitles.py:render_caption_overlays_simple'),
(6, 'Caption text jumps horizontally (each PNG had different width)', 'UNIFIED WIDTH: all PNGs are CAPTION_MAX_WIDTH + 40px wide', 'v4_subtitles.py:render_caption_png'),
(7, 'English words mispronounced by Russian TTS voice (e.g. "API" read as "а-пи-и")', 'Transliterate English words to Russian BEFORE TTS (API→АПИ, GPT-5→ДЖИПИТИ-ПЯТЬ)', 'tts_translit.py:transliterate_for_tts');

-- ============================================================
-- ALTHEA YouTube Shorts Pipeline v5.0 — NEW TABLES
-- ============================================================
-- Generated: 2026-08-08
-- Reason: v5 pipeline (SRT subtitles, queue, local LLM, news collector)
-- User: "прочитай что у тебя в рабочем репозитории и обнови SQL"
-- ============================================================


-- ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- 1. SRT SUBTITLE CONFIGURATION (v4.4 — replaces PNG captions)
-- ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- User: "текст караоке кривой почему он пляшет от слова к слову"
-- Fix: SRT + libass subtitles filter with Alignment=2 (fixed anchor)
-- ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS youtube_srt_config (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
    name VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    font_name VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'Inter',
    font_size INTEGER DEFAULT 56,
    bold BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1,
    primary_color VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT '&H00FFFFFF',     -- ASS &HAABBGGRR (white)
    back_color VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT '&H80000000',        -- 50% opacity black box
    border_style INTEGER DEFAULT 4,                      -- 4=box, 1=outline
    alignment INTEGER DEFAULT 2,                         -- 2=bottom-center (numpad)
    margin_l INTEGER DEFAULT 80,
    margin_r INTEGER DEFAULT 80,
    margin_v INTEGER DEFAULT 0,                          -- 0=default (removed 320 bug)
    max_chars_per_line INTEGER DEFAULT 38,
    max_words_per_phrase INTEGER DEFAULT 6,
    caption_lead_ms INTEGER DEFAULT -150,                -- show 150ms before audio
    phrase_tail_ms INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    min_phrase_duration_s REAL DEFAULT 0.8,
    max_phrase_duration_s REAL DEFAULT 3.5,
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1,
    notes TEXT,
    updated_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

INSERT INTO youtube_srt_config (id, name, font_size, primary_color, back_color, border_style, alignment, margin_v, notes) VALUES
(1, 'default', 56, '&H00FFFFFF', '&H80000000', 4, 2, 0, 'Bloomberg-style: white text on semi-transparent black box, bottom-center, fixed anchor (no jumping)'),
(2, 'large', 64, '&H00FFFFFF', '&H80000000', 4, 2, 0, 'Larger font for emphasis videos'),
(3, 'minimal', 48, '&H00FFFFFF', '&H00000000', 1, 2, 0, 'Outline only, no box (cleaner look)');


-- ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- 2. PER-CATEGORY SUBTITLE STYLES
-- ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- Each category can have its own subtitle color accent
-- (matches brand colors: AMAGI=amber, ALTHEA=green, etc.)
-- ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS youtube_subtitle_styles (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
    category VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    accent_color VARCHAR(20),                            -- e.g. &H00F5A623 for AMAGI amber
    text_color VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT '&H00FFFFFF',
    box_color VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT '&H80000000',
    font_size INTEGER DEFAULT 56,
    notes TEXT
);

INSERT INTO youtube_subtitle_styles (id, category, accent_color, text_color, box_color, font_size, notes) VALUES
(1, 'amagi',     '&H00F5A623', '&H00FFFFFF', '&H80000000', 56, 'AMAGI amber accent'),
(2, 'althea',    '&H002DD4A7', '&H00FFFFFF', '&H80000000', 56, 'ALTHEA green accent'),
(3, 'scio',      '&H004CC3E8', '&H00FFFFFF', '&H80000000', 56, 'Scio blue accent'),
(4, 'ctc',       '&H00C084FC', '&H00FFFFFF', '&H80000000', 56, 'CTC purple accent'),
(5, 'industry',  '&H00A8B8D4', '&H00FFFFFF', '&H80000000', 56, 'Industry muted accent'),
(6, 'contest',   '&H0022D3EE', '&H00FFFFFF', '&H80000000', 56, 'Contest cyan accent');


-- ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- 3. DAILY QUEUE SLOTS (6 runs per day, every 4 hours)
-- ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- User: "5 направление под них время выхода в эфир выпуск видео
--        по теме, давай сделаем разные промты"
-- 6-й слот = конкурсный (best of remaining)
-- ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS youtube_queue_slots (
    run_index INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,                       -- 1-6
    category VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,                       -- amagi/althea/scio/ctc/industry/contest
    hour_utc INTEGER NOT NULL,                           -- 0, 4, 8, 12, 16, 20
    hour_msk INTEGER NOT NULL,                           -- 3, 7, 11, 15, 19, 23
    description TEXT,
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1
);

INSERT INTO youtube_queue_slots (run_index, category, hour_utc, hour_msk, description) VALUES
(1, 'amagi',    0,  3,  'AMAGI — AI Safety / Cybersec'),
(2, 'althea',   4,  7,  'ALTHEA — Biotech / CRISPR'),
(3, 'scio',     8,  11, 'Scio — Deepfake / Trust'),
(4, 'ctc',      12, 15, 'CTC — MedAI / Diagnostics'),
(5, 'industry', 16, 19, 'Industry — DeepTech news'),
(6, 'contest',  20, 23, 'Contest — best of remaining');


-- ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- 4. DAILY QUEUE STATE (runtime state — committed to repo)
-- ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS youtube_queue_state (
    date_msk DATE PRIMARY KEY,
    run_index INTEGER NOT NULL,
    category VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending',                -- pending/assigned/published/failed
    news_id VARCHAR(200),
    news_title TEXT,
    news_url TEXT,
    actual_category VARCHAR(20),                         -- may differ from slot category (fallback)
    assigned_at TIMESTAMP,
    published_at TIMESTAMP,
    youtube_id VARCHAR(50),
    error TEXT,
    FOREIGN KEY (run_index) REFERENCES youtube_queue_slots(run_index)
);


-- ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- 5. LLM SCORE CACHE (avoid re-scoring same news)
-- ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS youtube_score_cache (
    title_hash VARCHAR(64) PRIMARY KEY,                  -- md5(title[:100])
    title TEXT NOT NULL,
    score INTEGER DEFAULT 5,
    relevance INTEGER DEFAULT 2,
    appeal INTEGER DEFAULT 2,
    freshness INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    uniqueness INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    reason TEXT,
    llm_category VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'industry',
    scored_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);


-- ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- 6. LOCAL LLM REGISTRY (QVikhr-3-1.7B and others)
-- ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- User: "Можем установить маленькую llm модель прямо на гитхаб?"
-- ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS youtube_llm_models (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    model_file VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    model_url TEXT NOT NULL,
    model_size_mb INTEGER,
    n_ctx INTEGER DEFAULT 4096,
    n_threads INTEGER DEFAULT 2,
    max_tokens INTEGER DEFAULT 300,
    temperature REAL DEFAULT 0.3,
    chat_template VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'chatml',          -- chatml, llama2, mistral
    requires_think_block BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1,              -- QVikhr noreasoning needs think block
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT 0,
    notes TEXT
);

INSERT INTO youtube_llm_models (id, name, model_file, model_url, model_size_mb, chat_template, requires_think_block, is_active, notes) VALUES

(2, 'Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct', 'qwen2.5-1.5b-instruct-q4_k_m.gguf', 'https://huggingface.co/Qwen/Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct-GGUF/resolve/main/qwen2.5-1.5b-instruct-q4_k_m.gguf', 990, 'chatml', 0, 0, 'Alternative — multilingual, slightly faster but worse Russian'),
(3, 'Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct', 'Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct-Q4_K_M.gguf', 'https://huggingface.co/bartowski/Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct-GGUF/resolve/main/Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct-Q4_K_M.gguf', 750, 'llama3', 0, 0, 'Fallback — smallest, but weak Russian');

    tone TEXT,
    opening_instruction TEXT,
    example_text TEXT,
    max_chars INTEGER DEFAULT 400,
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1,
    UNIQUE(category, prompt_type)
);

    keywords TEXT,                                       -- comma-separated filter keywords
    check_at TEXT,                                       -- comma-separated MSK hours
    rhythm VARCHAR(100),
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1,
    notes TEXT
);

-- ============================================================
-- ALTHEA YouTube Shorts Pipeline v5.1 — EXTRA UPLOAD + QUOTA
-- ============================================================
-- Generated: 2026-08-08
-- Reason: extra_upload.py rewritten to v5 pipeline (no althea-tech.ru,
--         no cloud LLM). Count-based quota tracking.
-- User: "Сделай все что необходимо обнови sql"
-- ============================================================


-- ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- 1. EXTRA UPLOAD CONFIGURATION (v5)
-- ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- extra_upload.py v5 settings — uses local LLM + news_collector
-- ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS youtube_extra_upload_config (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
    name VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    max_extra_per_run INTEGER DEFAULT 2,           -- max extra uploads per pipeline run
    absolute_max_per_day INTEGER DEFAULT 6,        -- hard cap per day (10,000 quota / 1,600 per upload)
    cooldown_seconds INTEGER DEFAULT 30,           -- pause between uploads
    use_v5_pipeline BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1,             -- use news_collector + local_llm (was v3: althea-tech.ru)
    use_local_llm_only BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1,          -- no cloud LLM (Groq/Gemini were 403/429)
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1,
    notes TEXT,
    updated_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

INSERT INTO youtube_extra_upload_config (id, name, max_extra_per_run, absolute_max_per_day, cooldown_seconds, notes) VALUES
(1, 'default', 2, 6, 30, 'v5: local LLM + news_collector. Count-based quota (real API check was 403).'),
(2, 'conservative', 1, 4, 60, 'Conservative: 1 extra per run, 4/day max'),
(3, 'aggressive', 3, 6, 15, 'Aggressive: 3 extra per run (risky for quota)');


-- ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- 2. DAILY UPLOAD TRACKING (for quota management)
-- ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- Tracks how many videos uploaded per day (count-based quota)
-- ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS youtube_upload_dates (
    date VARCHAR(10) PRIMARY KEY,                  -- YYYY-MM-DD
    upload_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    quota_units_used INTEGER DEFAULT 0,            -- estimated (upload_count × 1600)
    quota_units_remaining INTEGER DEFAULT 10000,   -- 10000 - used
    first_upload_at TIMESTAMP,
    last_upload_at TIMESTAMP,
    notes TEXT
);

-- Sample data (today)
INSERT OR IGNORE INTO youtube_upload_dates (date, upload_count, quota_units_used, quota_units_remaining, notes) VALUES
(date('now'), 0, 0, 10000, 'Fresh day, no uploads yet');


-- ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- 3. QUOTA HISTORY (track usage over time)
-- ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS youtube_quota_history (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    date VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
    checked_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    upload_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    quota_remaining INTEGER,                       -- from API (NULL if 403)
    quota_total INTEGER DEFAULT 10000,
    check_method VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'count',      -- 'api' or 'count'
    error TEXT                                     -- e.g. 'HTTP 403 Forbidden'
);


-- ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- 4. DIALOG ROLES (INTRO_TEMPLATES + SIGNIFICANCE_PHRASES)
-- ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- From llm_filter.py — 10 intros + 4-6 significance per category
-- User: "У нас был файл в котором много диалоговых ролей найди его"
-- ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS youtube_dialog_roles (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
    category VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    role_type VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,                -- 'intro' or 'significance'
    style VARCHAR(30),                             -- 'question', 'urgency', 'drama', 'personal', 'bold', 'hypothetical', 'neutral'
    text TEXT NOT NULL,
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1,
    UNIQUE(category, role_type, text)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS youtube_secret_requirements (
    name VARCHAR(50) PRIMARY KEY,
    description TEXT,
    is_required BOOLEAN DEFAULT 0,                 -- 1 = pipeline fails without it
    is_present BOOLEAN DEFAULT 0,                  -- 1 = added to GitHub Secrets
    used_in_v5 BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1,                  -- 1 = used by v5 pipeline
    notes TEXT
);

);
